# FMV play guide — Titan top 550 and all games rated 8–10/10 for FMV

Updated October 27, 2026. Ranked by the project's Titan scores on that date.

This catalog supports a comedy-driven video series across Sega CD, 3DO, PlayStation and PC. Each game below includes purchase access, My Abandonware status, console choices and an edition recommendation. The separate recording plan selects games for episode quality and production practice, without using budget or current ownership as priorities.

## Prices and editions

- **Digital prices** are USD unless another currency is shown. Steam prices marked October 27 were checked for the US market; sales can end. Other linked storefronts do not imply the same price. A store listing without a quoted price is not a price estimate.
- **Console prices** are rounded complete-in-box estimates from the project's October 20–25, 2026 PriceCharting snapshots unless an entry gives a newer observation date; they are not current seller quotes. The linked price pages let you check editions and recent transactions. Hardware, shipping, tax and accessories are extra.
- **My Abandonware: Download** means the named edition has game files on its listing. **Store-only** means the listing directs you to a purchase instead. **Page only / download removed** means the listing remains but its game download is unavailable. **Not found** means no matching listing was located; **No download verified** leaves the download status unresolved. Different platform versions may have different status.
- **PS1** means original PlayStation. Japanese/European imports need compatible hardware and may have language barriers. Owning a PS5 does not provide a way to play PS1 discs.
- **Recommendations** choose a practical version for this playthrough. They are not claims that every port was tested locally. Double Switch, Sewer Shark and Microcosm comparisons remain provisional. No capture setup has yet been recorded as tested in the project kit.
- **Arcade emulation** requires separately obtained game/video data. An emulator's existence does not establish that a particular game's laserdisc data or a working setup is available.

The ranking and physical estimates come from [the project catalog](../data/games.json). The [32-game recording plan](fmv-play-order.md) chooses main challenge versions and comparison material for the series. Ownership remains recorded in the tracker, but does not determine this sequence.

## Start playing

The [series recording order](fmv-play-order.md) keeps a 32-game opening rotation within the reviewed pre-2000 scope, with Double Switch replacing Her Story at #31. The [finite checklist](rip-fmv-checklist.md) defines the total current set; this broader purchase guide retains modern originals and hybrids as research for separate series. Production practice still comes before major classics:

1. **Tomcat Alley — Sega CD:** practice building a mission, failures, training and an outcome into a finished cut.
2. **Burning Soldier — 3DO:** practice action coverage and commentary with a different visual style.
3. **Midnight Raiders — Sega CD:** practice the affectionate bad-game voice and an honest verdict.
4. **Wirehead**, then **Sewer Shark:** the next published branching-movie and recognizable flagship episodes after the first three.

**Dragon’s Lair moves to recording slot 12.** Finish and review at least two complete edits before using its first attempt. Night Trap is the next major version-comparison event at #16. The three practice productions are planned published episodes 1–3. Recording and comparison pickups can happen out of public order.

## Purchase plan

Acquire the editions, comparison discs and peripherals that make the episode work. Prices below remain useful dated references, but neither budget nor current ownership controls the sequence. Secure rare games early if necessary without recording them before the format is ready.

The [acquisition plan](fmv-play-order.md#acquisition-plan-prepare-episodes-not-spending-waves) groups required releases by production milestone. Mad Dog McCree and Crime Patrol call for a tested 3DO gun/CRT setup; Dragon’s Lair and Night Trap call for matched comparison footage across editions. The source entries below retain their Titan ranks.

## Titan ranks 401 to 450

**LaserActive setup for this batch:** Original hardware needs the matching Sega PAC for Mega-LD or NEC PAC for LD-ROM². The [implementer’s emulation guide](https://techdocs.exodusemulator.com/Console/PioneerLaserActive/Software.html) requires firmware and a full `.mmi` capture containing analog video/audio and digital data. It flags standard ares v148 as broken and links a fixed build. These exact game setups have not been tested locally.

## Remaining games with FMV ratings of 8 to 10

These nine entries retain their original Titan ranks; intervening ranks are not included. Together with ranks 1–550 above, they cover all **315 ranked games rated 8–10/10 for FMV** in the current project catalog. The FMV rating measures video’s role in the game, rather than review quality.
